{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nZ3-based weighted partial MAX-SAT solver for constraint consensus.\\nInput: multi-trace constraint lists (list of list of dicts)\\nOutput: optimal satisfiable constraint subset, excluded contradictions, per-answer scores\\nDependencies: z3-solver\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport logging\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\n\\nimport z3\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n_BOOL_INDICATORS = re.compile(r'\\\\bImplies\\\\s*\\\\(|==\\\\s*True|==\\\\s*False|\\\\bNot\\\\s*\\\\(|\\\\bAnd\\\\s*\\\\(|\\\\bOr\\\\s*\\\\(')\\n\\n\\ndef _is_boolean_formula(formula_str: str) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Detect whether a formula uses boolean logic (vs arithmetic).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return bool(_BOOL_INDICATORS.search(formula_str))\\n\\n\\ndef parse_z3_formula(formula_str: str, var_type: str = \\\"auto\\\") -> z3.ExprRef | None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Safely parse a string into a z3 expression.\\n\\n    Supports:\\n      - Arithmetic: +, -, *, /, **, ==, !=, <, >, <=, >= (Real variables)\\n      - Boolean: Implies, And, Or, Not, == True/False (Bool variables)\\n    Auto-declares unknown variables as z3.Real or z3.Bool based on context.\\n    Returns None on parse failure.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not formula_str or not formula_str.strip():\\n        return None\\n\\n    formula_str = formula_str.strip()\\n\\n    use_bool = (var_type == \\\"bool\\\") or (var_type == \\\"auto\\\" and _is_boolean_formula(formula_str))\\n\\n    tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\\\b([a-zA-Z_]\\\\w*)\\\\b', formula_str))\\n    reserved = {'True', 'False', 'And', 'Or', 'Not', 'Implies', 'If', 'abs', 'Bool', 'Real'}\\n    var_names = tokens - reserved\\n\\n    ns: dict = {}\\n    for name in var_names:\\n        ns[name] = z3.Bool(name) if use_bool else z3.Real(name)\\n\\n    ns['And'] = z3.And\\n    ns['Or'] = z3.Or\\n    ns['Not'] = z3.Not\\n    ns['Implies'] = z3.Implies\\n    ns['If'] = z3.If\\n    ns['Bool'] = z3.Bool\\n    ns['Real'] = z3.Real\\n    ns['True'] = True\\n    ns['False'] = False\\n\\n    try:\\n        result = eval(formula_str, {\\\"__builtins__\\\": {}}, ns)\\n        if isinstance(result, (z3.ExprRef, z3.BoolRef)):\\n            return result\\n        if isinstance(result, bool):\\n            return z3.BoolVal(result)\\n        return None\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        logger.debug(\\\"Failed to parse z3 formula '%s': %s\\\", formula_str, e)\\n        return None\\n\\n\\ndef check_equivalence(f1: z3.ExprRef, f2: z3.ExprRef, timeout_ms: int = 1000) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if two z3 formulas are logically equivalent (f1 ⟺ f2).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    s = z3.Solver()\\n    s.set(\\\"timeout\\\", timeout_ms)\\n    s.add(z3.Not(f1 == f2))\\n    result = s.check()\\n    return result == z3.unsat\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass UniqueConstraint:\\n    expression: str\\n    z3_formula: z3.ExprRef\\n    weight: int  # cross-trace frequency\\n    source_traces: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\\n        return f\\\"UniqueConstraint(expr='{self.expression}', w={self.weight}, traces={self.source_traces})\\\"\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass MaxSATResult:\\n    satisfied: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    excluded: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    total_weight: int\\n    solve_time_ms: float\\n\\n\\nclass ConstraintDeduplicator:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Deduplicate constraints across traces using z3 equivalence checking.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, equiv_timeout_ms: int = 1000):\\n        self.equiv_timeout_ms = equiv_timeout_ms\\n\\n    def deduplicate(self, all_constraints: list[list[dict]]) -> list[UniqueConstraint]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Merge equivalent constraints across K traces.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            all_constraints: K lists of constraint dicts, one per trace.\\n        Returns:\\n            Deduplicated list with frequency weights.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        unique: list[UniqueConstraint] = []\\n\\n        for trace_idx, trace_constraints in enumerate(all_constraints):\\n            for c in trace_constraints:\\n                z3_f = parse_z3_formula(c.get(\\\"z3_formula\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n                if z3_f is None:\\n                    logger.debug(\\\"Skipping unparseable constraint: %s\\\", c.get(\\\"z3_formula\\\"))\\n                    continue\\n\\n                merged = False\\n                for uc in unique:\\n                    try:\\n                        if check_equivalence(uc.z3_formula, z3_f, self.equiv_timeout_ms):\\n                            uc.weight += 1\\n                            if trace_idx not in uc.source_traces:\\n                                uc.source_traces.append(trace_idx)\\n                            merged = True\\n                            break\\n                    except Exception:\\n                        continue\\n\\n                if not merged:\\n                    unique.append(UniqueConstraint(\\n                        expression=c.get(\\\"expression\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                        z3_formula=z3_f,\\n                        weight=1,\\n                        source_traces=[trace_idx],\\n                    ))\\n\\n        return unique\\n\\n\\nclass MaxSATSolver:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Weighted partial MAX-SAT solver using z3.Optimize.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def solve(self, unique_constraints: list[UniqueConstraint], timeout_ms: int = 10000) -> MaxSATResult:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Find the maximum-weight satisfiable subset of constraints.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not unique_constraints:\\n            return MaxSATResult(satisfied=[], excluded=[], total_weight=0, solve_time_ms=0.0)\\n\\n        opt = z3.Optimize()\\n        opt.set(\\\"timeout\\\", timeout_ms)\\n\\n        # Create indicator variables for each constraint\\n        indicators = []\\n        for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\\n            ind = z3.Bool(f\\\"__ind_{i}\\\")\\n            indicators.append(ind)\\n            if not z3.is_bool(uc.z3_formula):\\n                continue\\n            opt.add(z3.Implies(ind, uc.z3_formula))\\n            # maximize weighted sum\\n            opt.add_soft(ind, weight=uc.weight)\\n\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        result = opt.check()\\n        solve_time = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000\\n\\n        satisfied = []\\n        excluded = []\\n\\n        if result in (z3.sat, z3.unknown):\\n            model = opt.model()\\n            for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\\n                val = model.evaluate(indicators[i], model_completion=True)\\n                if z3.is_true(val):\\n                    satisfied.append(uc)\\n                else:\\n                    excluded.append(uc)\\n        else:\\n            # unsatisfiable — all excluded\\n            excluded = list(unique_constraints)\\n\\n        total_w = sum(uc.weight for uc in satisfied)\\n        return MaxSATResult(\\n            satisfied=satisfied,\\n            excluded=excluded,\\n            total_weight=total_w,\\n            solve_time_ms=solve_time,\\n        )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"}

variables)\\n      - Boolean: Implies, And, Or, Not, == True/False (Bool variables)\\n    Auto-declares unknown variables as z3.Real or z3.Bool based on context.\\n    Returns None on parse failure.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not formula_str or not formula_str.strip():\\n        return None\\n\\n    formula_str = formula_str.strip()\\n\\n    use_bool = (var_type == \\\"bool\\\") or (var_type == \\\"auto\\\" and _is_boolean_formula(formula_str))\\n\\n    tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\\\b([a-zA-Z_]\\\\w*)\\\\b', formula_str))\\n    reserved = {'True', 'False', 'And', 'Or', 'Not', 'Implies', 'If', 'abs', 'Bool', 'Real'}\\n    var_names = tokens - reserved\\n\\n    ns: dict = {}\\n    for name in var_names:\\n        ns[name] = z3.Bool(name) if use_bool else z3.Real(name)\\n\\n    ns['And'] = z3.And\\n    ns['Or'] = z3.Or\\n    ns['Not'] = z3.Not\\n    ns['Implies'] = z3.Implies\\n    ns['If'] = z3.If\\n    ns['Bool'] = z3.Bool\\n    ns['Real'] = z3.Real\\n    ns['True'] = True\\n    ns['False'] = False\\n\\n    try:\\n        result = eval(formula_str, {\\\"__builtins__\\\": {}}, ns)\\n        if isinstance(result, (z3.ExprRef, z3.BoolRef)):\\n            return result\\n        if isinstance(result, bool):\\n            return z3.BoolVal(result)\\n        return None\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        logger.debug(\\\"Failed to parse z3 formula '%s': %s\\\", formula_str, e)\\n        return None\\n\\n\\ndef check_equivalence(f1: z3.ExprRef, f2: z3.ExprRef, timeout_ms: int = 1000) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if two z3 formulas are logically equivalent (f1 ⟺ f2).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    s = z3.Solver()\\n    s.set(\\\"timeout\\\", timeout_ms)\\n    s.add(z3.Not(f1 == f2))\\n    result = s.check()\\n    return result == z3.unsat\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass UniqueConstraint:\\n    expression: str\\n    z3_formula: z3.ExprRef\\n    weight: int  # cross-trace frequency\\n    source_traces: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\\n        return f\\\"UniqueConstraint(expr='{self.expression}', w={self.weight}, traces={self.source_traces})\\\"\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass MaxSATResult:\\n    satisfied: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    excluded: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    total_weight: int\\n    solve_time_ms: float\\n\\n\\nclass ConstraintDeduplicator:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Deduplicate constraints across traces using z3 equivalence checking.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, equiv_timeout_ms: int = 1000):\\n        self.equiv_timeout_ms = equiv_timeout_ms\\n\\n    def deduplicate(self, all_constraints: list[list[dict]]) -> list[UniqueConstraint]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Merge equivalent constraints across K traces.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            all_constraints: K lists of constraint dicts, one per trace.\\n        Returns:\\n            Deduplicated list with frequency weights.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        unique: list[UniqueConstraint] = []\\n\\n        for trace_idx, trace_constraints in enumerate(all_constraints):\\n            for c in trace_constraints:\\n                z3_f = parse_z3_formula(c.get(\\\"z3_formula\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n                if z3_f is None:\\n                    logger.debug(\\\"Skipping unparseable constraint: %s\\\", c.get(\\\"z3_formula\\\"))\\n                    continue\\n\\n                merged = False\\n                for uc in unique:\\n                    try:\\n                        if check_equivalence(uc.z3_formula, z3_f, self.equiv_timeout_ms):\\n                            uc.weight += 1\\n                            if trace_idx not in uc.source_traces:\\n                                uc.source_traces.append(trace_idx)\\n                            merged = True\\n                            break\\n                    except Exception:\\n                        continue\\n\\n                if not merged:\\n                    unique.append(UniqueConstraint(\\n                        expression=c.get(\\\"expression\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                        z3_formula=z3_f,\\n                        weight=1,\\n                        source_traces=[trace_idx],\\n                    ))\\n\\n        return unique\\n\\n\\nclass MaxSATSolver:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Weighted partial MAX-SAT solver using z3.Optimize.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def solve(self, unique_constraints: list[UniqueConstraint], timeout_ms: int = 10000) -> MaxSATResult:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Find the maximum-weight satisfiable subset of constraints.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not unique_constraints:\\n            return MaxSATResult(satisfied=[], excluded=[], total_weight=0, solve_time_ms=0.0)\\n\\n        opt = z3.Optimize()\\n        opt.set(\\\"timeout\\\", timeout_ms)\\n\\n        # Create indicator variables for each constraint\\n        indicators = []\\n        for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\\n            ind = z3.Bool(f\\\"__ind_{i}\\\")\\n            indicators.append(ind)\\n            if not z3.is_bool(uc.z3_formula):\\n                continue\\n            opt.add(z3.Implies(ind, uc.z3_formula))\\n            # maximize weighted sum\\n            opt.add_soft(ind, weight=uc.weight)\\n\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        result = opt.check()\\n        solve_time = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000\\n\\n        satisfied = []\\n        excluded = []\\n\\n        if result in (z3.sat, z3.unknown):\\n            model = opt.model()\\n            for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\\n                val = model.evaluate(indicators[i], model_completion=True)\\n                if z3.is_true(val):\\n                    satisfied.append(uc)\\n                else:\\n                    excluded.append(uc)\\n        else:\\n            # unsatisfiable — all excluded\\n            excluded = list(unique_constraints)\\n\\n        total_w = sum(uc.weight for uc in satisfied)\\n        return MaxSATResult(\\n            satisfied=satisfied,\\n            excluded=excluded,\\n            total_weight=total_w,\\n            solve_time_ms=solve_time,\\n        )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"
    }
  }
}

re.compile(r'\\\\bImplies\\\\s*\\\\(|==\\\\s*True|==\\\\s*False|\\\\bNot\\\\s*\\\\(|\\\\bAnd\\\\s*\\\\(|\\\\bOr\\\\s*\\\\(')\\n\\n\\ndef _is_boolean_formula(formula_str: str) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Detect whether a formula uses boolean logic (vs arithmetic).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return bool(_BOOL_INDICATORS.search(formula_str))\\n\\n\\ndef parse_z3_formula(formula_str: str, var_type: str = \\\"auto\\\") -> z3.ExprRef | None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Safely parse a string into a z3 expression.\\n\\n    Supports:\\n      - Arithmetic: +, -, *, /, **, ==, !=, <, >, <=, >= (Real variables)\\n      - Boolean: Implies, And, Or, Not, == True/False (Bool variables)\\n    Auto-declares unknown variables as z3.Real or z3.Bool based on context.\\n    Returns None on parse failure.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not formula_str or not formula_str.strip():\\n        return None\\n\\n    formula_str = formula_str.strip()\\n\\n    use_bool = (var_type == \\\"bool\\\") or (var_type == \\\"auto\\\" and _is_boolean_formula(formula_str))\\n\\n    tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\\\b([a-zA-Z_]\\\\w*)\\\\b', formula_str))\\n    reserved = {'True', 'False', 'And', 'Or', 'Not', 'Implies', 'If', 'abs', 'Bool', 'Real'}\\n    var_names = tokens - reserved\\n\\n    ns: dict = {}\\n    for name in var_names:\\n        ns[name] = z3.Bool(name) if use_bool else z3.Real(name)\\n\\n    ns['And'] = z3.And\\n    ns['Or'] = z3.Or\\n    ns['Not'] = z3.Not\\n    ns['Implies'] = z3.Implies\\n    ns['If'] = z3.If\\n    ns['Bool'] = z3.Bool\\n    ns['Real'] = z3.Real\\n    ns['True'] = True\\n    ns['False'] = False\\n\\n    try:\\n        result = eval(formula_str, {\\\"__builtins__\\\": {}}, ns)\\n        if isinstance(result, (z3.ExprRef, z3.BoolRef)):\\n            return result\\n        if isinstance(result, bool):\\n            return z3.BoolVal(result)\\n        return None\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        logger.debug(\\\"Failed to parse z3 formula '%s': %s\\\", formula_str, e)\\n        return None\\n\\n\\ndef check_equivalence(f1: z3.ExprRef, f2: z3.ExprRef, timeout_ms: int = 1000) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if two z3 formulas are logically equivalent (f1 ⟺ f2).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    s = z3.Solver()\\n    s.set(\\\"timeout\\\", timeout_ms)\\n    s.add(z3.Not(f1 == f2))\\n    result = s.check()\\n    return result == z3.unsat\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass UniqueConstraint:\\n    expression: str\\n    z3_formula: z3.ExprRef\\n    weight: int  # cross-trace frequency\\n    source_traces: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\\n        return f\\\"UniqueConstraint(expr='{self.expression}', w={self.weight}, traces={self.source_traces})\\\"\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass MaxSATResult:\\n    satisfied: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    excluded: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    total_weight: int\\n    solve_time_ms: float\\n\\n\\nclass ConstraintDeduplicator:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Deduplicate constraints across traces using z3 equivalence checking.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, equiv_timeout_ms: int = 1000):\\n        self.equiv_timeout_ms = equiv_timeout_ms\\n\\n    def deduplicate(self, all_constraints: list[list[dict]]) -> list[UniqueConstraint]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Merge equivalent constraints across K traces.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            all_constraints: K lists of constraint dicts, one per trace.\\n        Returns:\\n            Deduplicated list with frequency weights.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        unique: list[UniqueConstraint] = []\\n\\n        for trace_idx, trace_constraints in enumerate(all_constraints):\\n            for c in trace_constraints:\\n                z3_f = parse_z3_formula(c.get(\\\"z3_formula\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n                if z3_f is None:\\n                    logger.debug(\\\"Skipping unparseable constraint: %s\\\", c.get(\\\"z3_formula\\\"))\\n                    continue\\n\\n                merged = False\\n                for uc in unique:\\n                    try:\\n                        if check_equivalence(uc.z3_formula, z3_f, self.equiv_timeout_ms):\\n                            uc.weight += 1\\n                            if trace_idx not in uc.source_traces:\\n                                uc.source_traces.append(trace_idx)\\n                            merged = True\\n                            break\\n                    except Exception:\\n                        continue\\n\\n                if not merged:\\n                    unique.append(UniqueConstraint(\\n                        expression=c.get(\\\"expression\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                        z3_formula=z3_f,\\n                        weight=1,\\n                        source_traces=[trace_idx],\\n                    ))\\n\\n        return unique\\n\\n\\nclass MaxSATSolver:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Weighted partial MAX-SAT solver using z3.Optimize.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def solve(self, unique_constraints: list[UniqueConstraint], timeout_ms: int = 10000) -> MaxSATResult:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Find the maximum-weight satisfiable subset of constraints.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not unique_constraints:\\n            return MaxSATResult(satisfied=[], excluded=[], total_weight=0, solve_time_ms=0.0)\\n\\n        opt = z3.Optimize()\\n        opt.set(\\\"timeout\\\", timeout_ms)\\n\\n        # Create indicator variables for each constraint\\n        indicators = []\\n        for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\\n            ind = z3.Bool(f\\\"__ind_{i}\\\")\\n            indicators.append(ind)\\n            if not z3.is_bool(uc.z3_formula):\\n                continue\\n            opt.add(z3.Implies(ind, uc.z3_formula))\\n            # maximize weighted sum\\n            opt.add_soft(ind, weight=uc.weight)\\n\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        result = opt.check()\\n        solve_time = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000\\n\\n        satisfied = []\\n        excluded = []\\n\\n        if result in (z3.sat, z3.unknown):\\n            model = opt.model()\\n            for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\\n                val = model.evaluate(indicators[i], model_completion=True)\\n                if z3.is_true(val):\\n                    satisfied.append(uc)\\n                else:\\n                    excluded.append(uc)\\n        else:\\n            # unsatisfiable — all excluded\\n            excluded = list(unique_constraints)\\n\\n        total_w = sum(uc.weight for uc in satisfied)\\n        return MaxSATResult(\\n            satisfied=satisfied,\\n            excluded=excluded,\\n            total_weight=total_w,\\n            solve_time_ms=solve_time,\\n        )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.53}}"}

Map answer -> set of trace indices that produced it\\n        answer_traces: dict[str, set[int]] = {}\\n        for t in traces:\\n            ans = str(t.get(\\\"answer\\\", \\\"\\\")).strip()\\n            idx = t.get(\\\"trace_idx\\\", 0)\\n            answer_traces.setdefault(ans, set()).add(idx)\\n\\n        scores: dict[str, float] = {}\\n        for cand in candidates:\\n            cand_key = str(cand).strip()\\n            supporting_traces = answer_traces.get(cand_key, set())\\n            weight = 0.0\\n            for uc in maxsat_result.satisfied:\\n                overlap = set(uc.source_traces) & supporting_traces\\n                if overlap:\\n                    weight += uc.weight\\n            scores[cand_key] = weight\\n\\n        return scores\\n\\n    def select_answer(\\n        self,\\n        scores: dict[str, float],\\n        answer_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the highest-scoring answer. I'm seeing that the error was actually caught by the outer handler in `run_full_mvp.py` rather than the pipeline's handler, which is why the result has an empty timing object and no traces. This is puzzling because Z3Exception should be caught by the generic `except Exception` clause in the pipeline, so somehow it's escaping that catch block even though it's a subclass of Exception.

= set(re.findall(r'\\b([a-zA-Z_]\\w*)\\b', formula_str))\n    reserved = {'True', 'False', 'And', 'Or', 'Not', 'Implies', 'If', 'abs', 'Bool', 'Real'}\n    var_names = tokens - reserved\n\n    ns: None\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Failed to parse z3 formula '%s': %s\", formula_str, e)\n        return None\n\n\ndef check_equivalence(f1: z3.ExprRef, f2: z3.ExprRef, timeout_ms: int = 1000) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Check if two z3 formulas are logically equivalent (f1 ⟺ f2).\"\"\"\n    if not z3.is_bool(f1) or not z3.is_bool(f2):\n        return False\n    s = z3.Solver()\n    s.set(\"timeout\", timeout_ms)\n    s.add(z3.Not(f1 == f2))\n    result = s.check()\n    return result == z3.unsat\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass UniqueConstraint:\n    expression: str\n f\"UniqueConstraint(expr='{self.expression}', w={self.weight}, traces={self.source_traces})\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass MaxSATResult:\n    satisfied: list[UniqueConstraint]\n    excluded: list[UniqueConstraint]\n    total_weight: int\n    solve_time_ms: float\n\n\nclass ConstraintDeduplicator:\n    \"\"\"Deduplicate constraints across traces using z3 equivalence checking.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, equiv_timeout_ms: int = 1000):\n        self.equiv_timeout_ms = equiv_timeout_ms\n\n    def deduplicate(self, all_constraints: list[list[dict]]) -> list[UniqueConstraint]:\n        \"\"\"Merge equivalent constraints across K traces.\n\n        Args:\n            all_constraints: K lists of constraint dicts, one per trace.\n        Returns:\n            Deduplicated list with frequency weights.\n        \"\"\"\n        unique: list[UniqueConstraint] = []\n\n        for trace_idx, trace_constraints in enumerate(all_constraints):\n            for c in trace_constraints:\n                z3_f = parse_z3_formula(c.get(\"z3_formula\", \"\"))\n                if z3_f is None:\n                    logger.debug(\"Skipping unparseable constraint: %s\", c.get(\"z3_formula\"))\n                    continue\n\n                merged = False\n                for uc in unique:\n                    try:\n                        if check_equivalence(uc.z3_formula, z3_f, self.equiv_timeout_ms):\n                            uc.weight += 1\n                            if trace_idx not in uc.source_traces:\n                                uc.source_traces.append(trace_idx)\n                            merged = True\n                            break\n                    except Exception:\n                        continue\n\n                if not merged:\n                    unique.append(UniqueConstraint(\n                        expression=c.get(\"expression\", \"\"),\n                        z3_formula=z3_f,\n                        weight=1,\n                        source_traces=[trace_idx],\n                    ))\n\n        return unique\n\n\nclass MaxSATSolver:\n    \"\"\"Weighted partial MAX-SAT solver using z3.Optimize.\"\"\"\n\n    def solve(self, unique_constraints: list[UniqueConstraint], timeout_ms: int = 10000) -> MaxSATResult:\n        \"\"\"Find the maximum-weight satisfiable subset of constraints.\"\"\"\n        if not unique_constraints:\n            return MaxSATResult(satisfied=[], excluded=[], total_weight=0, solve_time_ms=0.0)\n\n        opt = z3.Optimize()\n        opt.set(\"timeout\", timeout_ms)\n\n        indicators = []\n        active_mask = []\n        for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\n            ind = z3.Bool(f\"__ind_{i}\")\n            indicators.append(ind)\n            try:\n                if not z3.is_bool(uc.z3_formula):\n                    active_mask.append(False)\n                    continue\n                opt.add(z3.Implies(ind, uc.z3_formula))\n                opt.add_soft(ind, weight=uc.weight)\n                active_mask.append(True)\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Skipping constraint %d in MaxSAT: %s\", i, e)\n                active_mask.append(False)\n\n        if not any(active_mask):\n            return MaxSATResult(satisfied=[], excluded=list(unique_constraints),\n                                total_weight=0, solve_time_ms=0.0)\n\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\n        result = opt.check()\n        solve_time = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000\n\n        satisfied = []\n        excluded = []\n\n        if result in (z3.sat, z3.unknown):\n            model = opt.model()\n            for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\n                if not active_mask[i]:\n                    excluded.append(uc)\n                    continue\n                val = model.evaluate(indicators[i], model_completion=True)\n                if z3.is_true(val):\n                    satisfied.append(uc)\n                else:\n                    excluded.append(uc)\n        else:\n            excluded = list(unique_constraints)\n\n        total_w = sum(uc.weight for uc in satisfied)\n        return MaxSATResult(\n            satisfied=satisfied,\n            excluded=excluded,\n            total_weight=total_w,\n            solve_time_ms=solve_time,\n        )\nPYEOF",

### Assistant

_source line 76_

本地目录是项目管理目录，代码只在远程服务器上。修复已完成，总结如下：

## Bug 根因

`parse_z3_formula` 返回类型检查过宽：
```python
# 旧代码
if isinstance(result, (z3.ExprRef, z3.BoolRef)):
    return result
```
`ArithRef`（如 `x + y`、`x * 2`）是 `z3.ExprRef` 的子类，会通过检查。这些非布尔表达式传入 `z3.Implies(ind, ArithRef)` 时抛出 `Z3Exception: "Value cannot be converted into a Z3 Boolean value"`。

P2 的 LLM 约束提取产生了纯算术表达式（如变量名 `total`、算术 `n + m`），这些是 ArithRef 而非 BoolRef。

## 修复内容（3 处）

**1. `parse_z3_formula`**（源头过滤）：
```python
# 新代码：只接受布尔排序的表达式
if isinstance(result, z3.ExprRef) and z3.is_bool(result):
    return result
```

**2. `check_equivalence`**（防御性检查）：
```python
if not z3.is_bool(f1) or not z3.is_bool(f2):
    return False
```

**3. `MaxSATSolver.solve`**（安全网）：
- 增加 `active_mask` 跟踪哪些约束被实际加入优化器
- `Implies` 调用包裹 try/except
- 全部非布尔时直接返回空结果，不执行无意义的 `opt.check()`
- model 评估只检查 active 约束，非 active 直接归入 excluded

## 测试结果

35/35 通过，覆盖：
- 布尔表达式正常解析 ✓
- 非布尔表达式（ArithRef）被过滤 ✓
- 逻辑域公式（Implies/And/Or/Not）正常 ✓
- 边界情况（空串、None、纯常量）✓
- `check_equivalence` 排序安全 ✓
- P2 类混合约束不崩溃 ✓
- 全非布尔约束不崩溃 ✓
- P1/P3/P4 回归正常 ✓

## 重跑 P2

修复不需要重跑整个实验。如果 v2 实验仍在运行，修复已生效（代码已覆盖）。如果需要单独重跑 P2：

```bash
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
cd /root/symb_invariant_consensus
python run_full_mvp.py --mode vllm --k 12 --resume --output results/aime30_v2.json
```

`--resume` 会跳过已完成的题目，但 P2 由于 error 状态可能需要手动从 JSON 中删除 P2 条目后再 resume。
